(2.26)

(iii)

For all
\bar{x}\in B_{|x-c|}(c)
, the product of
these HPS converges to their Cauchy product:

\left(\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}\left(\bar{x}-c\right){}^{n}\right)\cdot\left(\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vph hoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\sum_{k=0}^{n}a_{k}b_{n-k}\left(\bar{x}-c\right){}^{n},

(2.27)

which is still a convergent HPS with radius of convergence greater
or equal to
\min(\text{\rm rad}\left(a_{n}\right)_{\rm c},\text{\rm rad}\left(b_{n}\right)_{\rm c})
.

(iv)

Let
\left[a_{n\varepsilon}\right]_{\text{c}}=(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}

and
\left[b_{n\varepsilon}\right]_{\text{c}}=(b_{n})_{{\rm c}}
be representatives
of the coefficients of the given HPS. Assume that
b_{0}=[b_{0\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}

is invertible, and recursively define (for
\varepsilon
small)
d_{0\varepsilon}:=\frac{a_{0\varepsilon}}{b_{0\varepsilon}}
,

d_{n\varepsilon}:=\frac{1}{b_{0\varepsilon}}\left(a_{n\varepsilon}-\sum_{l=1}^{n}b_{l\varepsilon}d_{n-l,\varepsilon}\right)\qquad\forall n\in\mathbb{N}_{>0}.

(2.28)

Then coefficients
(d_{n})_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{\text{\rm c}}
define a convergent
HPS with radius of convergence greater or equal to
\min(\text{\rm rad}\left(a_{n}\right)_{\rm c},\text{\rm rad}\left(b_{n}\right)_{\rm c})

such that for all
\bar{x}\in B_{|x-c|}(c)
, if
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}b_{n}\left(\bar{x}-c\right){}^{n}

is invertible, then

\frac{\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{ \scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}b_{n}\left(\bar{x}-c\right){}^{n}}=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}d_{n}\left(\bar{x}-c\right){}^{n}.

(2.29)

Proof.

Equalities (
2.25
) and (
2.26
) follow directly
from analogous properties of convergent hyperlimits, i.e.
[
23
, Sec. 5.2]
.
All the inequalities concerning the radius of convergence can be proved
in the same way from analogous results of the classical theory, because
of Def. .
(iii)
3
. For example, from Def.
8
.
(iii)

we have that both the ordinary series
\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}(x_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}

and
\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}b_{n\varepsilon}(x_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}
converge.
Thereby, their sum
\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}\left(a_{n\varepsilon}+b_{n\varepsilon}\right)(x_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}

converges with radius
\text{\rm rad}\left(a_{n}+b_{n}\right)_{{\rm c}{\varepsilon}}\geq\min\left(\text{\rm rad}\left(a_{n}\right)_{{\rm c}{\varepsilon}},\text{\rm rad}\left(b_{n}\right)_{{\rm c}{\varepsilon}}\right)
.
To prove (
2.27
) (assuming that
\bar{x}
lies in
the convergence set of the product HPS, see below), from Lem.
23

we have that both the series converge absolutely because
\bar{x}\in B_{|x-c|}(c)
.
We can hence apply the generalization of Mertens’ theorem to hyperseries
(see
[
29
, Thm. 37]
). To complete the proof of
(iii)
,
we start by showing that the terms
\left(\sum_{k=0}^{n}a_{k\varepsilon}b_{n-k,\varepsilon}\right)_{n,\varepsilon}

defines coefficients for an HPS. Let
(a_{n})_{\text{{\rm c}}}=\left[a_{n\varepsilon}\right]_{\text{{\rm c}}}
,

(b_{n})_{\text{{\rm c}}}=\left[b_{n\varepsilon}\right]_{\text{{\rm c}}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{\text{\rm c}}
,
so that:

\exists Q_{1},R_{1}\in\mathbb{N}\,\forall^{0}\varepsilon\,\forall n\in\mathbb{N}:\ \left|a_{n\varepsilon}\right|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-nQ_{1}-R_{1}}.

(2.30)

\exists Q_{2},R_{2}\in\mathbb{N}\,\forall^{0}\varepsilon\,\forall n\in\mathbb{N}:\ \left|b_{n\varepsilon}\right|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-nQ_{2}-R_{2}}.

(2.31)

Without loss of generality we can assume
Q_{2}>Q_{1}
.
We have

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{k=0}^{n}a_{k\varepsilon}b_{n-k,\varepsilon}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=0}^{n}|a_{k\varepsilon}||b_{n-k,\varepsilon}|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=0}^{n}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-kQ_{1}-R_{1}}.\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-(n-k)Q_{2}-R_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=0}^{n}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-kQ_{1}+kQ_{2}-nQ_{2}-R_{1}-R_{2}}.

(2.32)

We have
\rho_{\varepsilon}^{Q_{2}-Q_{1}}<1
because
Q_{2}>Q_{1}
,
and hence

\left|\sum_{k=0}^{n}a_{k\varepsilon}b_{n-k,\varepsilon}\right|\leq\frac{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-nQ_{2}-R_{1}-R_{2}}}{1-\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-Q_{1}+Q_{2}}}\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-nQ-R},

where
R:=R_{1}+R_{2}
and for a suitable
Q\in\mathbb{N}
(that can be
chosen uniformly with respect to
n\in\mathbb{N}
). Thereby, the product
HPS has well-defined coefficients and hence a suitable set of convergence.
Now, we want to show that
\bar{x}
lies in this set of convergence.
Since Def.
8
.
(i)
clearly holds
and Def.
8
.
(iii)
follows from Mertens’
Theorem (both
[
29
, Thm. 37]
and the classical version), it
remains to prove that we actually have a formal HPS (Def.
8
.
(ii)
)
and moderateness of derivatives (Def.
8
.
(iv)
).
The latter follows by the general Leibniz rule for the
k
-th derivative
of a product. For the former one, without loss of generality we can
assume
c=0
; let
\left(M_{\varepsilon}\right)
,
\left(N_{\varepsilon}\right)\in\mathbb{N}_{\sigma}
,
then for suitable
\left(\bar{M}_{\varepsilon}\right)
,
\left(\hat{M}_{\varepsilon}\right)\in\mathbb{N}_{\sigma}

and
\left(\bar{N}_{\varepsilon}\right)
,
\left(\hat{N}_{\varepsilon}\right)\in\mathbb{N}_{\sigma}

such that
M_{\varepsilon}=\bar{M}_{\varepsilon}+\hat{M}_{\varepsilon}
and
N_{\varepsilon}=\bar{N}_{\varepsilon}+\hat{N}_{\varepsilon}
,
we have

\left(\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\sum_{k=0}^{n}a_{n\varepsilon}b_{n-k,\varepsilon}\hat{x}_{\varepsilon}^{n}\right)=\left(\sum_{n=\bar{N}_{\varepsilon}}^{\bar{M}_{\varepsilon}}a_{n\varepsilon}\hat{x}_{\varepsilon}^{n}\right)\cdot\left(\sum_{n=\hat{N}_{\varepsilon}}^{\hat{M}_{\varepsilon}}b_{n,\varepsilon}\hat{x}_{\varepsilon}^{n}\right),

(2.33)

and thereby Def.
8
.
(ii)
follows.

(iv)
: To prove that
(d_{n})_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{\text{\rm c}}
,
without loss of generality, we can assume in (
2.30
)
and (
2.31
) that
Q_{1}=Q_{2}=:\hat{Q}>R_{1}=R_{2}=:\hat{R}

and
\hat{Q}>0
. By induction on
n\in\mathbb{N}
, we what to prove that

\forall^{0}\varepsilon\,\forall n\in\mathbb{N}:\ \left|d_{n\varepsilon}\right|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-n\hat{Q}-\hat{Q}}.

(2.34)

For
n=0
, we have
\left|d_{0\varepsilon}\right|=\left|\frac{a_{0\varepsilon}}{b_{0\varepsilon}}\right|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-\hat{R}+\hat{R}}\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-\hat{Q}}

for all
\varepsilon
because
\hat{Q}>0
. For the inductive step, we assume
(
2.34
) and use the recursive definition (
2.28
):

\displaystyle\left|d_{n+1,\varepsilon}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{a_{n+1,\varepsilon}}{b_{0\varepsilon}}\right|+\left|\frac{\sum_{l=1}^{n+1}b_{l\varepsilon}d_{n-l,\varepsilon}}{b_{0\varepsilon}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-(n+1)\hat{Q}-\hat{R}}\cdot\rho_{\varepsilon}^{\hat{R}}+\sum_{l=1}^{n+1}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-l\hat{Q}-\hat{R}}\cdot\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-(n-l)\hat{Q}-\hat{Q}}\cdot\rho_{\varepsilon}^{\hat{R}}

\displaystyle=\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-n\hat{Q}-\hat{Q}}+\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-n\hat{Q}-\hat{Q}}\leq 2\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-n\hat{Q}-\hat{Q}}.

We have
2\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-n\hat{Q}-\hat{Q}}\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-(n+1)\hat{Q}-\hat{Q}}

if and only if
2\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-\hat{Q}}
, which holds for
\varepsilon

small (independently by
n
). Finally, equality (
2.29
)
can be proved as we did above for the product because
\bar{x}\in B_{|x-c|}(c)

and

\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}\left(\bar{x}-c\right){}^{n}=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}d_{n}\left(\bar{x}-c\right){}^{n}.\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}b_{n}\left(\bar{x}-c\right){}^{n}.

(2.35)

From this equality, it also follows Def.
16
.
(ii)

because the product of a non-moderate net (on a co-final set) by a
moderate net cannot yield a moderate net. Finally, as above, moderateness
of derivatives follows from Mertens’ theorem and the
k
-th derivative
of the quotient.
∎

The following theorem concerns the composition of HPS:

Theorem 27
.

Let
(a_{n})_{\text{{\rm c}}}=\left[a_{n\varepsilon}\right]_{\text{{\rm c}}}
,

(b_{n})_{\text{{\rm c}}}=\left[b_{n\varepsilon}\right]_{\text{{\rm c}}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{\text{\rm c}}

be coefficients for HPS. Set
f(y):=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{ for all

and
g(x):=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{ for all

. Set

\displaystyle c_{0\varepsilon}

\displaystyle:=a_{0\varepsilon}

\displaystyle c_{n\varepsilon}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=0}^{+\infty}a_{k\varepsilon}\sum_{m_{1}+\ldots+m_{k}=n}b_{m_{1}\varepsilon}\cdot\ldots\cdot b_{m_{k}\varepsilon}\qquad\forall n\in\mathbb{N}_{>0}.

If
x\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(b_{n}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right)
and
g(x)\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(a_{n}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},b_{0}\right)

then
f(g(x))=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}c_{n}(x-c)^{n}
is a convergent
HPS.

Proof.

Since
\left[a_{n\varepsilon}\right]_{c}
,
\left[b_{n\varepsilon}\right]_{\text{{\rm c}}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{\text{\rm c}}

, we can assume that both (
2.2
) and (
2.3
)
hold with
\hat{Q}=Q_{1}=Q_{2}>0
and
\hat{R}=R_{1}=R_{2}>0
. We
have

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{k=0}^{n}a_{k\varepsilon}\sum_{m_{1}+\ldots+m_{k}=n}b_{m_{1}\varepsilon}\cdot\right.

\displaystyle\left.\ldots\cdot b_{m_{k}\varepsilon}\right|\leq\sum_{k=0}^{n}|a_{k\varepsilon}|\sum_{m_{1}+\ldots+m_{k}=n}|b_{m_{1}\varepsilon}|\cdot\ldots\cdot|b_{m_{k}\varepsilon}|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=0}^{n}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-k\hat{Q}-\hat{R}}\sum_{m_{1}+\ldots+m_{k}=n}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-m_{1}\hat{Q}-\hat{R}}\cdot\ldots\cdot\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-m_{k}\hat{Q}-\hat{R}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{n}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-k\hat{Q}-\hat{R}}\sum_{m_{1}+\ldots+m_{k}=n}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-n\hat{Q}-k\hat{R}}

\displaystyle=\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-\hat{R}}+\sum_{k=1}^{n}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-k\hat{Q}-\hat{R}}\sum_{m_{1}+\ldots+m_{k}=n}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-n\hat{Q}-k\hat{R}}

\displaystyle=\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-\hat{R}}+\sum_{k=1}^{n}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-k\hat{Q}-\hat{R}-n\hat{Q}-k\hat{R}}{n+k-1\choose k-1}

\displaystyle\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-\hat{R}}+2^{2n}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-\hat{R}-n\hat{Q}}\cdot\frac{1-\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-(n+1)(\hat{Q}+\hat{R})}}{1-\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-\hat{Q}-\hat{R}}}=:[*].

For
\varepsilon
small, we have
\frac{4}{\rho_{\varepsilon^{-1}}}\leq 1
, hence

\frac{2^{2n}}{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-n}}\leq 1
for the same
\varepsilon
and for
all
n\in\mathbb{N}
. Now, take
\varepsilon
small so that also
\frac{1}{1-\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-\hat{Q}-\hat{R}}}\leq 1
,
and
\frac{1}{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-1}}\leq\frac{1}{3}
. We hence have

[*]\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-\hat{R}}+\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-n\hat{Q}-\hat{R}-n}+\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-2n\hat{Q}-n\hat{R}-2\hat{R}-n}.

Since

\displaystyle\frac{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-\hat{R}}}{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-n(2\hat{Q}+\hat{R}+1)-2\hat{R}-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-1}}\leq\frac{1}{3}

\displaystyle\frac{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-n\hat{Q}-\hat{R}-n}}{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-n(2\hat{Q}+\hat{R}+1)-2\hat{R}-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-1}}\leq\frac{1}{3}

\displaystyle\frac{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-2n\hat{Q}-n\hat{R}-2\hat{R}-n}}{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-n(2\hat{Q}+\hat{R}+1)-2\hat{R}-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-1}}\leq\frac{1}{3},

we finally get

\forall^{0}\varepsilon\,\forall n\in\mathbb{N}:\ \left|\sum_{k=0}^{n}a_{k\varepsilon}\sum_{m_{1}+\ldots+m_{k}=n}b_{m_{1}\varepsilon}\cdot\ldots\cdot b_{m_{k}\varepsilon}\right|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-n(2\hat{Q}+\hat{R}+1)-2\hat{R}-1},

which proves that
(c_{n\varepsilon})_{n,\varepsilon}
defines coefficients for
an HPS. To prove that
x\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(c_{n}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right)
, we can proceed as
follows: Def.
8
.
(i)
can be
proved like in the classical case; Def.
8
.
(ii)

is a consequence of composition of polynomials if
M_{\varepsilon}<+\infty

or it can be proved proceeding like in the case of composition of
GSF if
M_{\varepsilon}=+\infty
: Def.
8
.
(iii)

and Def.
8
.
(iv)
can be proved
like for GSF (see
[
13
]
and Thm.
28
below).
∎

3.
Generalized real analytic functions and their calculus

A direct consequence of Def.
8
of set of convergence
is the following

.

for all

. Then
f\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\mathcal{GC}^{\infty}\left(\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(a_{n}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right),\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}\right)

.

Before defining the notion of GRAF, we need to prove that the derived

Theorem 29
.

\sigma\leq\rho^{*}
,

\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-18.0278pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-18.0278pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-18.0278pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-18.0278pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}_{>0}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}_{>0}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}_{>0}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\ is the same as the set of convergence of the original HPS
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}(x-c)^{n}

. Thereby, recursively, all the derivatives has the same set of convergence
of the original HPS and define a GSF.

Proof.

By Def.
3
.
(iii)
of radius of convergence
and the classical theory, we have

\displaystyle\text{\rm rad}\left(a_{n}\right)_{{\rm c}{\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle=\left(\limsup_{n\to+\infty}\left|a_{n\varepsilon}\right|^{1/n}\right)^{-1}=\left(\limsup_{n\to+\infty}\left|(n+1)a_{n+1,\varepsilon}\right|^{1/n+1}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle=\text{\rm rad}\left((n+1)a_{n+1}\right)_{{\rm c}{\varepsilon}},

so Def.
8
.
(i)
for the original
HPS and the derived one are equivalent. From the condition
\left[a_{n\varepsilon}\cdot\left(x_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}\right]_{\text{{\rm s}}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}\llbracket x-c\rrbracket

and
\sigma\leq\rho^{*}
, in the usual way it follows that
\left[(n+1)a_{n+1,\varepsilon}\cdot\left(x_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}\right]_{\text{{\rm s}}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}\llbracket x-c\rrbracket
.
Vice versa, from
(n+1)\left|a_{n+1,\varepsilon}\right|\geq\left|a_{n+1,\varepsilon}\right|

the opposite implication follows. The condition Def.
8
.
(iv)

about moderateness of derivatives for the original HPS clearly implies
the analogue condition for the derived one. For the opposite inclusion,
we can distinguish the case
x=_{\text{s}}c
or
|x-c|>0
, the former
one being trivial. We have

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{n=1}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}n(x_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n-1}\right|

\displaystyle=|x_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon}|^{-1}\left|\sum_{n=1}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}n(x_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right|

\displaystyle\geq|x_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon}|^{-1}\left|\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}(x_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right|,

so that also the net
\left(\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}(x_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right)\in\mathbb{R}_{\rho}

if the derivative is moderate.
∎

28
motivates the following definition:

Example 31
.

(a)

Clearly, if
(a_{n})_{c}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{\text{\rm c}}
,

, and we set
f(x)=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\ U

is contained in the union of all the sharp interior sets
\text{int}\left(\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(a_{n}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right)\right)
,
because of condition
(i)
.

(b)

Example
15
shows that Dirac

\delta
is entire at
0
but it is not at any
c\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
such
that
\left|c\right|\geq s\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
for some
s
.

(c)

Example
12
of a function
f

with a flat point shows that
f
is a GRAF, but if
c=0
, then
s\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}

satisfying condition
(i)
is infinitesimal, whereas
if
c\gg 0
, then
s\gg 0
is finite, and these two types of set of
convergence are always disjoint.

Corollary 32
.

Let
\sigma\leq\rho^{*}
,
U\subseteq\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}

be a sharply open set and
f\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\mathcal{GC}^{\omega}(U,\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}})
, then also
f^{\prime}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\mathcal{GC}^{\omega}(U,\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}})

and it can be computed with the derived HPS.

Because of our definition Def.
8
of
set of convergence, several classical results can be simply translated
in our setting considering the real analytic function that defines
a given GRAF.

Theorem 33
.

Let
\sigma\leq\rho^{*}
,
\left(a_{n}\right)_{\text{\emph{c}}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{\text{\rm c}}
,

c\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
, and set
f(x)=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}(x-c)^{n}

for all interior points
x\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(a_{n}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right)
, then
a_{k}=\frac{f^{(k)}(c)}{k!}

for all
k\in\mathbb{N}
.

Proof.

From Cor.
32
, we have
f^{(k)}(x)=\left[\sum_{n=k}^{\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}k!{n\choose k}(x_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n-k}\right]

for all the interior points
x\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(a_{n}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right)
. For
x=c

(which is always a sharply interior point because of Thm.
9
)
this yields the conclusion.
∎

Corollary 34
.

Let
\sigma\leq\rho^{*}
,
U
be a sharply
open set of
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
, and
f\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\mathcal{GC}^{\omega}(U,\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}})
. Then for all
c\in U

the Taylor coefficients
\left(\frac{f^{(n)}(c)}{n!}\right)_{\text{c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{\text{\rm c}}
.

The definition of
1
-dimensional integral of GSF by using
primitives, allows us to get a simple proof of the term by term integration
of GRAF:

Theorem 35
.

In the assumptions of the previous theorem,
set

F(x):=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\frac{a_{n}(x-c)^{n+1}}{n+1}

for all the interior points
x\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(a_{n}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right)
. Then
F(x)=\int_{c}^{x}f(x)\,\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}x}{\hbox{\rm d}x}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}x$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}x$}}

and
F
is a GRAF on the interior points of
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(a_{n}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right)
.

Proof.

The proof that
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\frac{a_{n}(x-c)^{n+1}}{n+1}

is a convergent HPS with the same set of convergence of
f
can be
done as in Thm.
29
, and hence
F
is a GRAF on
the interior points of
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(a_{n}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right)
. The remaining part of
the proof is straightforward by using Cor.
32
, so
that
F^{\prime}(x)=f\left(x\right)
and
F(c)=0
. and using
[
13
, Thm. 42, Def. 43]
.
∎

We close this section by first noting that, differently
with respect to the classical theory, if
f(x)=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}(x-c)^{n}

for all
x\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(a_{n}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right)
, and we take another point
\bar{c}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(a_{n}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right)
,
we do not have that
(\bar{c}-\text{\rm rad}\left(a_{n}\right)_{\rm c}+|c-\bar{c}|,\bar{c}+\text{\rm rad}\left(a_{n}\right)_{\rm c}-|c-\bar{c}|)\subseteq\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(a_{n}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right)
;
in fact for
c=\bar{c}
this would yield the false equality
(c-\text{\rm rad}\left(a_{n}\right)_{\rm c},c+\text{\rm rad}\left(a_{n}\right)_{\rm c})=\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(a_{n}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right)
.
On the other hand, in the following result we show that
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(\frac{f^{(n)}(\bar{c})}{n!}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},\bar{c}\right)\subseteq\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(\frac{f^{(n)}(c)}{n!}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right)
:

Theorem 36
.

In the assumptions of Thm.
33
,
if
\bar{c}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(\frac{f^{(n)}(c)}{n!}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right)
, then
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(\frac{f^{(n)}(\bar{c})}{n!}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},\bar{c}\right)\subseteq\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(\frac{f^{(n)}(c)}{n!}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right)
.

Proof.

In fact, since
\bar{c}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(\frac{f^{(n)}(c)}{n!}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},c\right)
, we
have

f^{(n)}(\bar{c})=\ \mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\,\,\hskip-18.0278pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\,\,\hskip-18.0278pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\,\,\hskip-18.0278pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\,\,\hskip-18.0278pt$}$}}}\sum_{m\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}_{\geq n}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\,\,\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\,\,\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\,\,\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\,\,\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{m\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}_{\geq n}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\,\,\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\,\,\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\,\,\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\,\,\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{m\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}_{\geq n}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\,\,\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\,\,\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\,\,\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\,\,\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{m\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}_{\geq n}\,}}\frac{f^{(m)}(c)}{m!}n!{m\choose n}(x-c)^{m-n}.

Thereby, if
x\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(\frac{f^{(n)}(\bar{c})}{n!}\right)_{\text{\rm c}},\bar{c}\right)
,
we have

\displaystyle f(x)

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\frac{f^{(n)}(\bar{c})}{n!}(x-\bar{c})^{n}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\frac{(x-\bar{c})^{n}}{n!}\cdot\ \mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\,\,\,\hskip-18.0278pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\,\,\,\hskip-18.0278pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\,\,\,\hskip-18.0278pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\,\,\,\hskip-18.0278pt$}$}}}\sum_{m\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}_{\geq n}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\,\,\,\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\,\,\,\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\,\,\,\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\,\,\,\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{m\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}_{\geq
